# V1 phase execution ledger — 2026-10-22 (parent orchestration)

## 2026-10-28 12:20 UTC — push gate: source/typecheck/Rust all green; blocked on one pre-existing test

`bun install --frozen-lockfile --offline` (pinned bun, cache-backed) repaired a partially
materialised `node_modules`: `packages/core/node_modules/effect` was a broken symlink and
`node_modules/.bin/tsgo` did not exist, so typecheck and several suites could not resolve their
dependencies. 4697 packages installed; no tracked file changed.

With that repaired, `scripts/architecture/run_push_gate.sh` passes: source audits
(`validate_convergence` 1302 checks / 0 failures, `validate_external_sources` 439 / 0,
`audit_workspace_packages` 0 errors, `audit_overlay_visibility` `hidden_new_source=0`,
`audit_markdown_repo` `ACTIVE_BLOCKERS 0`), HorizonCode typecheck, `cargo build` of both pinned
bins, `managed_control` and `resource_budget` Rust regressions, the Core suites (including
`kernel-projection-live` **1 pass** after the `wrk`-prefixed fixture fix) and every OpenCode suite
through `session/tool-batch`.

**Blocker (step 18/18): `packages/opencode/test/session/prompt.test.ts -t "V1 model write is
admitted"`** — `timed out waiting for Rust Guard approval`. Established by experiment:

- It is **pre-existing**: the version before this session's first commit does not even compile
  against the current schema (`Expected HorizonAgentProfileV2.Revision, got 1`), so this gate step
  was already red before this work.
- No `GuardApprovalRequested` is ever published for that write, because the kernel returns
  `status: "prepared"` through the documented quiet direct-mode path
  (`mode_approval_candidate` -> `approve_direct_mode_batch` -> one-use lease). The write *does*
  reach Rust: it returns a real `effectID` (`eff_cbc07984…`) and `receiptDigest`
  (`blake3:0629fa00…`), so Guard is admitted through, not bypassed.
- Opting the session into `approvalPolicy: "always"` does emit `guard.approval.requested` and
  `guard.approval.resolved`, but the following tool dispatch then fails:
  `stage=effect, hostCode=effect_denied, reason="approval principal, owner epoch, or one-use state
  does not match"` -> `Tool batch denied: Rust Guard unavailable`. The interactive approval flow
  for **model tool calls** is therefore not wired end to end; only quiet admission works today.
- This test is covered by neither CI (`.github/workflows/horizoncode-full-validation.yml`) nor
  `v1-final-verify.sh`.

The push hook was **not** bypassed: 24 commits remain local and the working tree is clean.

## 2026-10-28 03:20 UTC — terminal containment landed; the whole tool_batch_guard_matrix passes

`GUARDED_PTY_RUNTIME_READY` is now `true` and every terminal.v1 run/start ingress is live with
durable ExecutionHost containment. Six defects were found by turning the gate on, in the order the
kernel hit them:

1. **cgroup-v2 child scan rejected control files** (`cgroup_v2::count_directory_entries`): a cgroup
   directory always exposes `cgroup.procs`, `cgroup.kill`, `cpu.stat`… next to its children, so every
   root-run bind failed with "managed execution cgroup contains an invalid entry". Dot-carrying
   names are now skipped (control files); a bare name that is not an exact 64-hex execution digest
   is still rejected, so the retained-cgroup bound and the tamper check are unchanged.
2. **terminal output artifacts were unpublishable**: the capture was filed with
   `ArtifactRedactionV1::Unknown`, which the store refuses by contract. Observed output is now
   classified `NotNeeded` (workspace-scoped, reads still require a principal-bound permit).
3. **receipt encoding rejected `Value::Signed(0)`**: the terminal settlement receipt used the signed
   MessagePack variant for a nonnegative exit code, so every successful run returned
   `invalid_effect_request` ("terminal request is malformed"). Canonical rule now applied:
   nonnegative → `Unsigned`, negative → `Signed` (same rule the existing `exit_code_value` helper
   already documents in `host_kernel.rs`).
4. **run continuations were not mode-typed**: `terminal.v1/run` requires the same `mode` field the
   approved call carried; the matrix fixtures sent the pre-mode shape and were rejected at
   `invalid_effect_request` before lease consumption.
5. **`FinalizeRun` over-validated Task evidence**: the reducer required every Task's typed
   independent proof to sit at the run's frozen final revision, which contradicts the per-Task-head
   contract the managed fixtures encode (Task A verified at `sha('a')`, Task B at `sha('b')`, run
   finalizes at `sha('b')`). The strict conjunct is removed; each Task's `verified_revision` must
   still match its own evidence, and the run-level verifier proof is what must match the frozen
   revision. Regression split into `finalize_run_accepts_per_task_proofs_at_their_own_observed_revisions`
   (was a false negative) and `finalize_run_rejects_a_task_proof_whose_verified_revision_disagrees_with_its_evidence`.
6. **UNKNOWN diagnostics were swallowed**: the cgroup-bind and artifact-publication refusals now
   print the exact containment reason, so a fail-closed UNKNOWN is debuggable instead of opaque.

Evidence (root, WSL `6.18.54.1-microsoft-standard-WSL2`, Landlock ABI 7, bubblewrap 0.9.0):
- `tool_batch_guard_matrix` **9/9** (`--test-threads=1`, 13.55 s): cross-session lease reuse denied
  with no spawn, command-mutation after approval denied and lease consumed, `files.write` lease
  cannot execute `terminal.run`, mixed batch never starts a process, **workspace write confined +
  outside write denied + `.git/config` write denied**, and **host TCP listener unreachable from the
  confined command** (`NETWORK_DENIED`, `listener.accept()` fails).
- `host_terminal_process` **3/3**, `host_terminal_events_process` **1/1** (owner-loss escapee cleanup),
  `tool_batch_multi_call` **8/8**, `managed_control` integration **39/39** and lib **57/57**.
- `scripts/verify/v1-rust-verify.sh` gained `run_step_root`: containment steps (04, new 08) run with
  the privilege a non-delegated cgroup hierarchy needs, against a separate `/tmp` target so a
  privileged build never leaves root-owned artifacts in `kernel/target`.

Committed with the repo-hygiene work: `.gitignore` now covers generated build/runtime scratch
(target dirs, sandboxes, embedded repo snapshots, bun/npm caches, temp logs), so test scratch can
never be mistaken for hidden source by `audit_overlay_visibility.py`. AGENTS.md regained the
DOC-009/DOC-012 governance/plan/provenance pointers the push gate requires, and the
`agents-sdk` fixture's six missing reference targets were created so `audit_markdown_repo.py`
reports `ACTIVE_BLOCKERS 0`.

## 2026-10-27 21:10 UTC — GUARDED WRITE JOURNEY PASSES

- **Root cause of the lease mismatch:** `executeWrite`/`executePatch` re-registered a Location from a workspace-less `Location.Ref` instead of reusing the Location the ToolBatch had already bound the prepared leases to, so the kernel's `consume_lease` saw a different `locationID`. Both settlement paths now reuse `locationBySession` (the batch's Location) with the previous registration only as a fallback.
- **E2E harness:** the message parser only inspected the final assistant message, so a successful write (tool step) followed by a `DONE` text step reported `write=none`. It now scans every assistant message for the newest write tool part.
- **Result — real journey, zero egress:** `E2E_V1_SMOKE=PASS`, `auth_negative=401 no_file_created=true`, `session=ses_…`, `model=opencode/e2e-offline`, `catalog=inline-loopback zero_egress=true`, `assistant_error=none cost=0 write=completed`, file `.tmp/e2e/ok-…txt content=OK bytes=2`. HostKernel authorized the exact Guarded write, the effect settled with a receipt, and the file was created with the requested bytes.
- Supporting evidence: `workspace_revision` **7/7**, `--lib effect` **63/63**, core typecheck exit 0, adapter 3/3, focused regression 85/85.

## 2026-10-27 21:05 UTC — symlink-safe workspace revision; guarded write reaches the lease stage

- **Workspace revision no longer refuses symbolic links:** links are hashed by their target string and never followed; dependency/build/cache trees (`node_modules`, `target`, `target-p2`, `dist`, `.turbo`, `.bun`, `.cache`, `__pycache__`, `.git`) are excluded so a real project with installed packages stays inside the bounded walk. Checkpoint observation (`require_single_link`) still refuses links. Regression `workspace_revision_hashes_symlink_targets_without_following_them` asserts: editing the link's target does not change the digest, a dependency tree does not change it, and repointing the link does. `cargo test --lib workspace_revision` **7/7**; `--lib effect` **63/63**.
- **E2E harness corrected twice:** the generated kernel wrapper now `process.chdir`s into the served project (previously `bun run --cwd packages/opencode` left the instance root at `packages/opencode`, so repository-root writes were correctly denied), and the guarded-write journey now runs in the run's own `work/` project instead of this vendored monorepo (138k files / 5.7 GiB outside dependency trees, above the 100k-file / 1 GiB revision bound).
- **Guarded write now reaches lease settlement.** Blocker chain observed in order, each step fixed or explained: `location_owner_conflict` → `workspace revision cannot include symbolic links` → `workspace exceeds bounded revision verification limits` → `path_outside_or_unsafe` (harness cwd) → current **`Session supervisor effect failed: capability lease binding mismatch`** (kernel `consume_lease` identity check). This is the next focused debug; no file was written and the run stayed fail-closed throughout.
- Free-model turn unchanged: the allowlisted catalog entry fails the strict free proof (`cost {input:0, output:0, cache:{}}`), so the route is not registered and the CLI reports `ProviderModelNotFoundError`; no provider request.

## 2026-10-27 20:55 UTC — local workspace binding; E2E advances past owner conflict

- **Default local workspace (new):** every direct Session must be workspace-bound or HostKernel refuses direct-session authority. Added `LocalAdapter` (`packages/opencode/src/control-plane/adapters/local.ts`, registered as `local`) that routes to the project directory and never creates, moves, deletes, or rewrites it, and `Session.create` now persists/reuses a deterministic `wrk_local_<sha>` row when the instance has no workspace. Adapter regression **3/3**; focused regression (`direct-free-credential`, `managed-control`, `tool/write`) **85/85**; opencode typecheck **exit 0**.
- **Offline guarded-write E2E advanced:** the previous `location_owner_conflict` is gone and the session is created with `workspaceID=wrk_local_25354aa…`. The next host refusal is `effect_denied` — "workspace revision cannot include symbolic links" — because the E2E instance root is the repository, which contains `node_modules/...` symlinks. Any project with installed dependencies therefore cannot run direct-session effects under the current revision walker.
- **Free-model turn now reaches provider resolution:** the session is workspace-bound, and the turn fails closed with `ProviderModelNotFoundError: openrouter/nvidia/nemotron-3.5-super-vl:free`. Instrumentation (reverted) showed the exact cause: the allowlisted catalog entry has `cost {input:0, output:0, cache:{}}`, so `isFreeModel` returns false (incomplete cache pricing is unknown, not zero) and the route is never registered. No provider request or paid call was made. Fixing this is a policy decision: either accept provider-declared free input/output for the exact allowlist or keep the strict proof and surface a named "not provably free" error.
- Remaining blockers: symlink policy in `workspace_snapshot_with_hook`; the free-proof/allowlist decision above; terminal containment (`tool_batch_guard_matrix` 3/9).

## 2026-10-27 20:40 UTC — guarded credential import works; two real defects fixed

- **`secret.v1/authorize_put` rejected as `malformed`:** the Core messagepack decoder widens the kernel's unsigned u64 `expiresAtUnixMs` to `bigint`, while both authorizers required `number`. Fixed in `credential-mutation-authorizer.ts` (`positiveTimestamp`) and `credential-use-authorizer.ts` (bounded `unsignedMs` normalization of `issuedAt`/`expiresAt`/`committedAtUnixMs`/`redeemedAt`). Regressions cover the put grant and the use lease/redemption. Focused **19/19**, full `test/auth` **110/110**, opencode typecheck **exit 0**.
- **Secret Service session could never be established:** the code negotiated `dh-ietf1024-sha256-aes128-cbc-pkcs7` but used the **768-bit RFC 2409 group-1** prime, so every real 128-byte service public key was rejected as out of range. Fixed to the 1024-bit group-2 prime with fixed-width 128-byte client/shared values; new regression asserts 128-byte public keys and rejects an all-zero peer key.
- **Environment:** WSL had no Secret Service provider. Installed `gnome-keyring` + `libsecret-tools` (system packages) and unlocked a session keyring; `org.freedesktop.secrets` is present on the user bus.
- **VERIFIED product path:** `horizoncode provider --provider openrouter` driven through a masked PTY with the repo-local HostKernel manifest reports **"Credential stored for OpenRouter (openrouter) at version 1."** The credential lives in the Secret Service entry written by the guard path; the test-only copy sits in gitignored `.env` (mode 0600) as requested.
- **Leak scan:** the key appears only in `.env`; no copy in the repo, `.tmp`, `reports`, app data or logs.
- **NOT VERIFIED — free-model turn:** `horizoncode run --model openrouter/nvidia/nemotron-3.5-super-vl:free` returns a generic server error; the app log shows the session created with `workspaceID=undefined`, so the turn never reaches the provider gate. This is the same root cause as the offline E2E write failure (`location_owner_conflict`); a default local-workspace bootstrap is the prerequisite for both journeys.

## Shared contracts (parent-decided)

1. Durable OwnerLog/journal payload representation is `serde_json::Value`; `messagepack::Value` is the private RPC wire representation only. Convert at the boundary with helpers owned by P2 (`kernel/src/messagepack.rs`).
2. `kernel/src/host_kernel.rs` is P2-only. `kernel/src/effect_host.rs` is P1-only. P1 requests HostKernel changes through the parent/P2; P2 requests EffectHost changes through the parent/P1.
3. CodeMode mixed waves: effect calls use ordinary Guard leases and exact receipts; `host_callback` calls receive no synthetic lease and only bounded redacted output projection/digest. HostKernel binds before leases; failure/ambiguity stays fail-closed.
4. P2 owns HostKernel and message/protocol DTOs; P1 owns Shell/Guard/EffectHost/ExecutionHost; P7 owns Core `runner/llm.ts` and TUI; P6 owns provider transport/catalog; P3 owns context compile; P8 owns resource/worktree primitives.
5. No phase edits `HANDOFF.md`, `CURRENT_RUN.md`, `implementationtov1release.md`, `arch_V1/**`, root `package.json`, or `bun.lock`. Parent updates trackers.
6. No git write operations by phase agents. Parent owns staging/commits/push.
7. **Authorized exception to rule 5 (parent, 2026-10-24):** P9's exact `bun.lock` importer bin-path
   correction (`./bin/opencode` → `./bin/horizoncode` for the `packages/opencode` importer, two path
   strings only) is ratified — it is test-first under
   `test_workspace_lockfile_importers_match_manifest_identity` (RED then GREEN) and is required for
   `bun install --frozen-lockfile` to match the manifest. No resolution, integrity or dependency row
   may be changed; any further `bun.lock` edit needs explicit parent approval.
8. **P1-10 fence ruling (parent, 2026-10-24):** the post-read `directSessionWorkspaceSnapshot`
   revision fence is sufficient, so `ExecuteInput.readAdmission` is **not** added to the shared
   `Materialization.settle` boundary. The proposed shape stays on record in `reports/phases/P7.md`;
   raising it later requires parent sign-off plus a test proving the fence is insufficient.
9. **P1-10 client seam:** only `prepare_tool_batch_read` may pass a private `repo_read` context
   through `HostKernelClientImpl.request`; every other `effects.v2` operation under that context stays
   denied, and an optional caller-supplied envelope `requestID` is permitted only for this path.
